## Instruction Priority

Superpowers skills override default system prompt behavior, but **user instructions always take precedence**:

1. **User's explicit instructions** (CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, AGENTS.md, direct requests) — highest priority
2. **Superpowers skills** — override default system behavior where they conflict
3. **Default system prompt** — lowest priority

If CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, or AGENTS.md says "don't use TDD" and a skill says "always use TDD," follow the user's instructions. The user is in control.

## How to Access Skills

**In Claude Code:** Use the `Skill` tool. When you invoke a skill, its content is loaded and presented to you—follow it directly. Never use the Read tool on skill files.

**In Copilot CLI:** Use the `skill` tool. Skills are auto-discovered from installed plugins. The `skill` tool works the same as Claude Code's `Skill` tool.

**In Gemini CLI:** Skills activate via the `activate_skill` tool. Gemini loads skill metadata at session start and activates the full content on demand.

**In other environments:** Check your platform's documentation for how skills are loaded.

## Platform Adaptation

Skills use Claude Code tool names. Non-CC platforms: see `references/copilot-tools.md` (Copilot CLI), `references/codex-tools.md` (Codex) for tool equivalents. Gemini CLI users get the tool mapping loaded automatically via GEMINI.md.

# Using Skills

## The Rule

**Invoke relevant or requested skills BEFORE any response or action.** Even a 1% chance a skill might apply means that you should invoke the skill to check. If an invoked skill turns out to be wrong for the situation, you don't need to use it.

```dot
digraph skill_flow {
    "User message received" [shape=doublecircle];
    "About to EnterPlanMode?" [shape=doublecircle];
    "Already brainstormed?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke brainstorming skill" [shape=box];
    "Might any skill apply?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke Skill tool" [shape=box];
    "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'" [shape=box];
    "Has checklist?" [shape=diamond];
    "Create TodoWrite todo per item" [shape=box];
    "Follow skill exactly" [shape=box];
    "Respond (including clarifications)" [shape=doublecircle];

    "About to EnterPlanMode?" -> "Already brainstormed?";
    "Already brainstormed?" -> "Invoke brainstorming skill" [label="no"];
    "Already brainstormed?" -> "Might any skill apply?" [label="yes"];
    "Invoke brainstorming skill" -> "Might any skill apply?";

    "User message received" -> "Might any skill apply?";
    "Might any skill apply?" -> "Invoke Skill tool" [label="yes, even 1%"];
    "Might any skill apply?" -> "Respond (including clarifications)" [label="definitely not"];
    "Invoke Skill tool" -> "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'";
    "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'" -> "Has checklist?";
    "Has checklist?" -> "Create TodoWrite todo per item" [label="yes"];
    "Has checklist?" -> "Follow skill exactly" [label="no"];
    "Create TodoWrite todo per item" -> "Follow skill exactly";
}
```

## Red Flags

These thoughts mean STOP—you're rationalizing:

| Thought | Reality |
|---------|---------|
| "This is just a simple question" | Questions are tasks. Check for skills. |
| "I need more context first" | Skill check comes BEFORE clarifying questions. |
| "Let me explore the codebase first" | Skills tell you HOW to explore. Check first. |
| "I can check git/files quickly" | Files lack conversation context. Check for skills. |
| "Let me gather information first" | Skills tell you HOW to gather information. |
| "This doesn't need a formal skill" | If a skill exists, use it. |
| "I remember this skill" | Skills evolve. Read current version. |
| "This doesn't count as a task" | Action = task. Check for skills. |
| "The skill is overkill" | Simple things become complex. Use it. |
| "I'll just do this one thing first" | Check BEFORE doing anything. |
| "This feels productive" | Undisciplined action wastes time. Skills prevent this. |
| "I know what that means" | Knowing the concept ≠ using the skill. Invoke it. |

## Skill Priority

When multiple skills could apply, use this order:

1. **Process skills first** (brainstorming, debugging) - these determine HOW to approach the task
2. **Implementation skills second** (frontend-design, mcp-builder) - these guide execution

"Let's build X" → brainstorming first, then implementation skills.
"Fix this bug" → debugging first, then domain-specific skills.

## Skill Types

**Rigid** (TDD, debugging): Follow exactly. Don't adapt away discipline.

**Flexible** (patterns): Adapt principles to context.

The skill itself tells you which.

## User Instructions

Instructions say WHAT, not HOW. "Add X" or "Fix Y" doesn't mean skip workflows.

name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

Claiming work is complete without verification is dishonesty, not efficiency.

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## Why This Matters

From 24 failure memories:
- your human partner said "I don't believe you" - trust broken
- Undefined functions shipped - would crash
- Missing requirements shipped - incomplete features
- Time wasted on false completion → redirect → rework
- Violates: "Honesty is a core value. If you lie, you'll be replaced."

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

## The Bottom Line

**No shortcuts for verification.**

Run the command. Read the output. THEN claim the result.

This is non-negotiable.

  → Map package files and relevant foundation docs/source
  • Audit source, schemas, exports, contracts, dependency usage, and file sizes
  • Run requested test/typecheck/lint/build commands where sandbox permits
  • Produce PASS/FAIL verdict with line-numbered findings

# CLAUDE.md

> **GENERATED FROM agent-contract.md — DO NOT EDIT BY HAND.**
>
> Edit `agent-contract.md` then run `pnpm generate:configs`. CI fails if drift detected.

This file is the Claude Code entry point for the **SelfKnowledgeBaseWeb** monorepo. It tells
every Claude session what the project is, the hard rules, the agent roster, and the workflow.

- Spec: [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-29-self-knowledge-base-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-29-self-knowledge-base-design.md)
- Active plan: [docs/plans/active.md](docs/plans/active.md)
- Single source for agents: [agent-contract.md](agent-contract.md)
- Codex tool invocations: [docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md](docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md)

## Hard rules

1. **File size**: 200 lines (target) / 300 lines (ESLint `max-lines` warn) / 500 lines
   (`pnpm size-check` hard fail). Spec §3.6.
2. **Cross-file references**: every doc/code change must keep doc cross-references and
   contract files in sync (`packages/*/CONTRACT.md`, `agent-contract.md`).
3. **Lychee link-check**: broken markdown links block merge. Run `pnpm link-check` locally.
4. **`pnpm check` is mandatory before requesting review** (lint + typecheck + test + build + size).
5. **No direct git from workers**: only `git-operator` may commit / branch / rebase / push.
6. **No web access from workers**: only `researcher` may `web_search` / `web_fetch`.
7. **No cross-package moves from workers**: only `refactorer` may reorganize packages,
   and every reorganization requires an ADR under `docs/decisions/`.

## Commands cheat-sheet

| Command                 | Purpose                                               |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| `pnpm install`          | Install workspace dependencies                        |
| `pnpm dev`              | Run all `dev` scripts (parallel)                      |
| `pnpm check`            | Lint + typecheck + test + build + size-check          |
| `pnpm check:affected`   | Same, scoped to packages affected since `origin/main` |
| `pnpm size-check`       | Hard 500-line limit on every source file              |
| `pnpm link-check`       | Lychee scan over `./**/*.md`                          |
| `pnpm generate:configs` | Re-derive everything from `agent-contract.md`         |
| `pnpm format`           | Prettier write across the repo                        |

## Review workflow (spec §3.2 + ADR-0007 D2)

```
worker (writes code)
   │
   ▼
[Bash] codex exec --profile code-reviewer  (Codex 5.3-spark, line-level rigor, cheap default)
   │
   ├── if high-risk (D2 rows 1/2/4/8) → [Bash] codex exec --profile pr-gate  (Codex 5.5, deep scan)
   │
   ▼
pr-reviewer    (Claude, selective per ADR-0007 D2; spec match + regression + arch consistency)
   │
   ▼
git-operator   (Claude, only authorized git surface, runs `pnpm check` once more)
```

High-risk triggers per ADR-0007 D2 (4 rows +pr-gate +pr-reviewer, 4 rows +pr-reviewer only):
- **+pr-gate +pr-reviewer** (D2 rows 1/2/4/8): contract change, package add/remove, new ADR required, CI/deploy/auth/security touch.
- **+pr-reviewer only, skip pr-gate** (D2 rows 3/5/6/7): spec/agent-contract/ADR edit, delete/rename package, cross ≥3 packages, performance-auditor flagged.

See [ADR-0007 D2](docs/decisions/ADR-0007-job-function-codex-heavy-execution.md).

Codex tools (code-reviewer / pr-gate / plan-challenger / 5 scaffolders) are
[orchestrator-direct Bash invocations](docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md) post ADR-0007 D5,
not teammate spawns.

     2	import type { MathMdastJsxElement, MathTiptapNode } from './serialize';
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * MDX 解析 stub。Wave 3 mdx-bridge 在 mdastBlockToTiptap 看到
     6	 * mdxJsxFlowElement{name:'Math'} 时按 mdxComponent 路由到本函数（RFC §5）。
     7	 *
     8	 * `display` 三种 MDX 形式 (mdast-util-mdx-jsx convention):
     9	 *   - `<Math display>` (boolean shorthand)        → attr.value === null  → true
    10	 *   - `<Math display="true">`                     → attr.value === "true" → true
    11	 *   - `<Math display="false">`                    → attr.value === "false"→ false
    12	 * 任何其他形式（数字 / 非 true/false 字符串）throw — 不静默 false。
    13	 *
    14	 * 其他 attr (如 expression) 一律期望 string；null shorthand 仅 display 适用。
    15	 */
    16	export function parseMath(node: MathMdastJsxElement): MathTiptapNode {
    17	  if (node.name !== mathCore.mdxComponent) {
    18	    throw new Error(
    19	      `parseMath: expected mdxComponent="${mathCore.mdxComponent}", got "${node.name}"`,
    20	    );
    21	  }
    22	  const rawProps: Record<string, string | boolean> = {};
    23	  for (const attr of node.attributes) {
    24	    if (attr.name === 'display') {
    25	      if (attr.value === null) {
    26	        rawProps[attr.name] = true;
    27	      } else if (attr.value === 'true' || attr.value === 'false') {
    28	        rawProps[attr.name] = attr.value === 'true';
    29	      } else {
    30	        throw new Error(
    31	          `parseMath: invalid display attribute value: ${JSON.stringify(attr.value)}`,
    32	        );
    33	      }
    34	    } else {
    35	      if (attr.value === null) {
    36	        throw new Error(
    37	          `parseMath: attribute "${attr.name}" must have a string value (got null shorthand; only "display" supports boolean shorthand)`,
    38	        );
    39	      }
    40	      rawProps[attr.name] = attr.value;
    41	    }
    42	  }
    43	  const validated = mathCore.propsSchema.parse(rawProps);
    44	  return {
    45	    type: 'math',
    46	    attrs: { expression: validated.expression, display: validated.display },
    47	  };
    48	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import { mathCore } from './core-definition';
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * MDX 序列化 stub。完整实现等 Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing table 落地（RFC §5）：
     5	 * mdx-bridge 拿到 TiptapNode{type:'math'} 时按 mdxComponent 字符串路由到本函数。
     6	 *
     7	 * Wave 2 阶段仅暴露稳定签名；mdx-bridge 还未引用此 export，所以 stub 抛错足够。
     8	 */
     9	export interface MathTiptapNode {
    10	  readonly type: 'math';
    11	  readonly attrs: { readonly expression: string; readonly display: boolean };
    12	}
    13	
    14	/**
    15	 * `value` is `string | null` per mdast-util-mdx-jsx convention:
    16	 *   - `<Math display>` (boolean shorthand) → attribute with `value: null`
    17	 *   - `<Math display="true">` / `<Math display="false">` → string
    18	 *   - `<Math expression="x^2">` → string (always)
    19	 * `parseMath` handles both null and string forms for the `display` attribute.
    20	 * `serializeMath` always emits string form (`String(boolean)`) for round-trip
    21	 * stability — null shorthand only on the parse side.
    22	 */
    23	export interface MathMdastJsxElement {
    24	  readonly type: 'mdxJsxFlowElement';
    25	  readonly name: string;
    26	  readonly attributes: ReadonlyArray<{
    27	    readonly type: 'mdxJsxAttribute';
    28	    readonly name: string;
    29	    readonly value: string | null;
    30	  }>;
    31	  readonly children: readonly unknown[];
    32	}
    33	
    34	export function serializeMath(node: MathTiptapNode): MathMdastJsxElement {
    35	  const validated = mathCore.propsSchema.parse(node.attrs);
    36	  return {
    37	    type: 'mdxJsxFlowElement',
    38	    name: mathCore.mdxComponent,
    39	    attributes: [
    40	      { type: 'mdxJsxAttribute', name: 'expression', value: validated.expression },
    41	      { type: 'mdxJsxAttribute', name: 'display', value: String(validated.display) },
    42	    ],
    43	    children: [],
    44	  };
    45	}

     1	import katex from 'katex';
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * Single authority for KaTeX rendering across this package (ADR-0006 item #5).
     5	 * Math.tsx (React/NodeView path) and Math.astro (SSR path) MUST both import
     6	 * `renderMath` from here — never inline `katex.renderToString` with their own
     7	 * options literal. The byte-equivalence between editor / SSR / NodeView output
     8	 * follows from this single import. ssr-render.test.ts exercises a corpus to
     9	 * detect drift if a future edit accidentally re-inlines the call.
    10	 *
    11	 * Options policy:
    12	 * - `displayMode`: caller-controlled (inline vs block).
    13	 * - `throwOnError: false`: KaTeX renders invalid LaTeX as `.katex-error` red box;
    14	 *   error-explicit (math.css binds `.katex-error` to `--color-error` token).
    15	 * - `output: 'html'`: HTML-only (no MathML); SSR + React DOM both consume HTML
    16	 *   string. Matches Math.astro's `set:html` path.
    17	 * - `strict: 'ignore'`: KaTeX warnings (deprecated commands, unicode chars) do
    18	 *   not throw; consistent with `throwOnError: false`.
    19	 */
    20	export const renderMath = (expression: string, display: boolean): string =>
    21	  katex.renderToString(expression, {
    22	    displayMode: display,
    23	    throwOnError: false,
    24	    output: 'html',
    25	    strict: 'ignore',
    26	  });

 succeeded in 108ms:
     1	import type { BlockViewProps } from '@skb/block-foundation';
     2	import { mathCore } from '../core/core-definition';
     3	import { renderMath } from './render-math';
     4	
     5	/**
     6	 * MathView 接受 inferred props ({ expression, display }) — 与
     7	 * `mathCore.propsSchema` 的 ZodInfer 等价。函数式组件而非
     8	 * `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<typeof ...>>` 显式标注，避免 generic 变 invariant
     9	 * 在 BlockRegistry.registerUI 处与 ZodTypeAny widening 冲突（exactOptionalPropertyTypes 触发）。
    10	 *
    11	 * KaTeX 渲染走 `./render-math.ts` 单一权威，与 Math.astro SSR path 同源
    12	 * （ADR-0006 item #5 algorithm replication）。错误态由 KaTeX 渲染为
    13	 * `.katex-error` 红色块（math.css 绑 `--color-error` token）。
    14	 */
    15	export function MathView({
    16	  props,
    17	}: BlockViewProps<typeof mathCore.propsSchema>): JSX.Element {
    18	  const html = renderMath(props.expression, props.display);
    19	  const Tag = props.display ? 'div' : 'span';
    20	  return (
    21	    <Tag
    22	      data-block="math"
    23	      data-display={props.display ? 'true' : 'false'}
    24	      // KaTeX 输出受信（npm 包内部生成），无 user HTML 注入路径
    25	      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }}
    26	    />
    27	  );
    28	}
    29	
    30	export const MathEditorView = MathView;
    31	export const MathRenderView = MathView;

    17	 */

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
     2	import { renderMath } from '../ui-default/render-math';
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * Byte-equality corpus for renderMath authority (ADR-0006 item #5).
     6	 *
     7	 * `renderMath` is the single import point for KaTeX in this package; both
     8	 * Math.tsx (React/NodeView path) and Math.astro (SSR path) call it. This
     9	 * suite exercises a representative input corpus to:
    10	 *   1. catch silent regressions if a future edit re-inlines `katex.renderToString`
    11	 *      with diverging options (drift detector for the algorithm-replication audit);
    12	 *   2. assert error-explicit behavior — invalid LaTeX must surface as
    13	 *      `.katex-error` in the output (spec D1 "red error box with the source
    14	 *      visible"; not silent fallback).
    15	 *
    16	 * KaTeX is the runtime authority — these tests do NOT pin the exact HTML byte
    17	 * shape (KaTeX patch versions can shift markup); they assert structural
    18	 * invariants (root class, displayMode discriminator, error-class presence)
    19	 * that any in-package option-divergence would break.
    20	 */
    21	describe('renderMath (KaTeX SSR authority)', () => {
    22	  it('renders inline expression with displayMode=false (no display block)', () => {
    23	    const html = renderMath('a^2 + b^2', false);
    24	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex"/);
    25	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    26	  });
    27	
    28	  it('renders display expression with displayMode=true (wraps in katex-display)', () => {
    29	    const html = renderMath('\\sum_{i=0}^n x_i', true);
    30	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    31	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex"/);
    32	  });
    33	
    34	  it('renders multiline displayed expression (multi-row aligned env)', () => {
    35	    const html = renderMath(
    36	      '\\begin{aligned} a &= 1 \\\\ b &= 2 \\end{aligned}',
    37	      true,
    38	    );
    39	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    40	    // multi-line rendering produces multiple mord/mrel spans
    41	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex"/);
    42	    expect(html.length).toBeGreaterThan(100);
    43	  });
    44	
    45	  it('renders malformed LaTeX with errorColor inline + source visible (throwOnError=false invariant)', () => {
    46	    const html = renderMath('\\notARealCommand{x}', false);
    47	    // KaTeX with throwOnError=false renders unknown commands inline with
    48	    // errorColor (default #cc0000); source string is preserved verbatim so the
    49	    // user can see the offending expression (spec D1 "red error box with the
    50	    // source visible"). math.css uses [data-block='math'] [style*="cc0000"]
    51	    // upgrade selector to bind --color-error token + bordered box.
    52	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex"/);
    53	    expect(html).toMatch(/notARealCommand/);
    54	    expect(html).toMatch(/#cc0000/);
    55	  });
    56	
    57	  it('inline + display same expression produce different output (displayMode discriminator)', () => {
    58	    const expr = '\\frac{1}{2}';
    59	    const inlineHtml = renderMath(expr, false);
    60	    const displayHtml = renderMath(expr, true);
    61	    expect(inlineHtml).not.toBe(displayHtml);
    62	    expect(displayHtml).toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    63	    expect(inlineHtml).not.toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    64	  });
    65	});

/bin/bash -lc 'nl -ba packages/block-foundation/src/registry.ts' in /home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import type { ZodTypeAny, infer as ZodInfer } from 'zod';
     2	import type { ComponentType } from 'react';
     3	
     4	export type BlockKind = 'prose' | 'component' | 'render' | 'viz';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * BlockCore：headless 层 —— 无 React 依赖，纯逻辑（props schema + MDX 序列化在
     8	 * mdx-bridge 处理）。Core 可被 Adopter 复用并配自定义 UI。
     9	 */
    10	export interface BlockCoreDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny = ZodTypeAny> {
    11	  /** kebab-case identifier，全局唯一 */
    12	  readonly name: string;
    13	  readonly kind: BlockKind;
    14	  readonly propsSchema: TSchema;
    15	  /** MDX 中实际使用的 PascalCase 组件名，例如 "Callout" / "JupyterBlock" */
    16	  readonly mdxComponent: string;
    17	}
    18	
    19	export interface BlockViewProps<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny> {
    20	  readonly props: ZodInfer<TSchema>;
    21	  readonly content?: string;
    22	  /** Wave 2 起追加 runtime context（kernel session / theme / etc.） */
    23	}
    24	
    25	/**
    26	 * BlockUI：presentational 层 —— React 组件，绑定到已注册的 core。
    27	 * 同一 core 可挂多个 UI（uiId 区分），Adopter 可写自己的 UI 替代 ui-default。
    28	 */
    29	export interface BlockUIDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny = ZodTypeAny> {
    30	  /** 必须指向已 registerCore 的 core */
    31	  readonly coreName: string;
    32	  /** UI 实现 id：'default' / 'minimal' / 用户自定义 */
    33	  readonly uiId: string;
    34	  readonly EditorView: ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>;
    35	  readonly RenderView: ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>;
    36	}
    37	
    38	export function defineCore<T extends ZodTypeAny>(
    39	  def: BlockCoreDefinition<T>,
    40	): BlockCoreDefinition<T> {
    41	  return def;
    42	}
    43	
    44	export function defineUI<T extends ZodTypeAny>(
    45	  def: BlockUIDefinition<T>,
    46	): BlockUIDefinition<T> {
    47	  return def;
    48	}
    49	
    50	/**
    51	 * BlockRegistry：双层注册表（ADR-0003）。
    52	 * registerCore：挂业务定义；registerUI：挂视觉实现。
    53	 * 同 core 多 UI 场景：getUI(name) 不带 uiId 时取首个注册的（约定为 'default'）。
    54	 */
    55	export class BlockRegistry {
    56	  readonly #cores = new Map<string, BlockCoreDefinition>();
    57	  readonly #uis = new Map<string, BlockUIDefinition[]>();
    58	
    59	  registerCore(core: BlockCoreDefinition): void {
    60	    if (this.#cores.has(core.name)) {
    61	      throw new Error(`Duplicate core name: ${core.name}`);
    62	    }
    63	    this.#cores.set(core.name, core);
    64	  }
    65	
    66	  registerUI(ui: BlockUIDefinition): void {
    67	    if (!this.#cores.has(ui.coreName)) {
    68	      throw new Error(`Unknown core for UI registration: ${ui.coreName}`);
    69	    }
    70	    const existing = this.#uis.get(ui.coreName) ?? [];
    71	    if (existing.some((u) => u.uiId === ui.uiId)) {
    72	      throw new Error(
    73	        `Duplicate UI registration: core=${ui.coreName} uiId=${ui.uiId}`,
    74	      );
    75	    }
    76	    existing.push(ui);
    77	    this.#uis.set(ui.coreName, existing);
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  getCore(name: string): BlockCoreDefinition | undefined {
    81	    return this.#cores.get(name);
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  listCores(): readonly BlockCoreDefinition[] {
    85	    return [...this.#cores.values()];
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  /** 不传 uiId 时返回首个（约定为 'default'）；传了精确匹配 */
    89	  getUI(coreName: string, uiId?: string): BlockUIDefinition | undefined {
    90	    const list = this.#uis.get(coreName);
    91	    if (!list || list.length === 0) return undefined;
    92	    if (uiId === undefined) return list[0];
    93	    return list.find((u) => u.uiId === uiId);
    94	  }
    95	
    96	  listUIs(coreName: string): readonly BlockUIDefinition[] {
    97	    return [...(this.#uis.get(coreName) ?? [])];
    98	  }
    99	}

     1	# @skb/block-math Contract
     2	
     3	KaTeX-backed math block (Wave 2 Track D1）。第一个 `render-block-eng` 包；
     4	hand-craft（不走 codex-block-generator），block-pdf (D2) 同 owner 独立 hand-craft。
     5	
     6	## Public surface
     7	
     8	四个 entry：
     9	
    10	- `.` (root barrel) — re-export `./core`
    11	- `./core` — headless 层
    12	  - `mathCore: BlockCoreDefinition<typeof propsSchema>` — name=`'math'` / kind=`'render'` / mdxComponent=`'Math'`
    13	  - `serializeMath(node) → mdxJsxFlowElement` — Tiptap → mdast (Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing 时启用)
    14	  - `parseMath(mdast) → TiptapNode` — mdast → Tiptap (同上)
    15	  - 类型: `MathTiptapNode` / `MathMdastJsxElement`
    16	- `./ui-default` — presentational 层
    17	  - `mathUiDefault: BlockUIDefinition<typeof mathCore.propsSchema>` — uiId=`'default'`
    18	  - `MathEditorView` / `MathRenderView` — `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<...>>`
    19	  - `renderMath(expression, display) → string` — KaTeX HTML 字符串生成器（**single authority** for editor + SSR + NodeView，定义在 `src/ui-default/render-math.ts`）
    20	  - `MATH_TOKENS: MathTokens` — design-token name witnesses（`@skb/design-tokens` 类型绑定）
    21	  - sibling: `Math.astro`（apps/site SSR 直接 import，亦走 `renderMath`）
    22	- `./ui-default/math.css` — 视觉规则单一来源（design-token-bound，error/fg color 走 `var(--color-*)`）
    23	
    24	### Consumer 使用方式
    25	
    26	`apps/site` / `editor-shell` 必须 `import '@skb/block-math/ui-default/math.css';`
    27	方能渲染 `.katex-error` 错误态样式以及 display/inline 间距 token。同时
    28	consumers 必须 `import 'katex/dist/katex.css';`（KaTeX 自身字体 + 内部布局；
    29	本包不重发包，避免重复加载）。
    30	
    31	`propsSchema` 形状（见 `src/core/core-definition.ts`）：
    32	
    33	```typescript
    34	z.object({
    35	  expression: z.string().min(1),
    36	  display: z.boolean().default(false),
    37	}).strict()
    38	```
    39	
    40	## Invariants
    41	
    42	继承自 [block-foundation/CONTRACT.md](../block-foundation/CONTRACT.md#invariants)：
    43	`Defensive copy` / `Schema strictness` / `Default UI lookup` / `Serialize / parse hook ownership`
    44	（即 `BlockRegistry.listCores()` / `listUIs(coreName)` defensive-copy 行为、
    45	`propsSchema` 必须 `.strict()`、同 `coreName` 多 UI 时 `getUI(coreName)` 取首注册 `uiId`、
    46	serialize/parse 由本包 `core/` own 不在 foundation 注册）。
    47	
    48	block-math 特定不变量：
    49	
    50	- **`coreName='math'` (kebab-case)** + **`mdxComponent='Math'` (PascalCase)**:
    51	  `mathCore.name`/`.mdxComponent` 字面值固定，与 BlockRegistry / mdx-bridge 路由 ([RFC §1](../block-foundation/RFC.md#1-core-side)) 一致
    52	- **Hook 命名 verb-as-prefix**: `serializeMath` / `parseMath` / `renderMath` ([RFC §5](../block-foundation/RFC.md#5-serialize--parse-hook-ownership-scope-clarification))
    53	- **`uiId='default'` reserved**: inherit foundation 的 `Default UI lookup` 不变量（首注册即 default）
    54	- **KaTeX 是 runtime authority** + **renderMath single authority**: `src/ui-default/render-math.ts`
    55	  唯一 wrap `katex.renderToString`（ADR-0006 item #5 algorithm replication —
    56	  authority = `katex` npm 包）。Math.tsx + Math.astro 都 import `./render-math`；
    57	  ssr-render.test.ts 4-row corpus 检测 inline 漂移
    58	- **Headless 自给**: `core/` 不 import React / Tiptap / KaTeX；仅依赖 `@skb/block-foundation` + `zod`（ADR-0003 D1）。`ui-default/` 才 import React + katex + design-tokens
    59	- **propsSchema single authority**: 仅在 `src/core/core-definition.ts` 定义；ui-default / serialize / parse / 测试均 import `mathCore.propsSchema`（ADR-0006 item #4）
    60	- **Self-validating serialize/parse**: `serializeMath` / `parseMath` 各自调 `propsSchema.parse`（parse 侧 display 先字符串 coerce → boolean）；契约偏离在 mdx-bridge 路由前 throw
    61	- **错误显式（不静默）**: KaTeX 配 `throwOnError: false` + `strict: 'ignore'`：
    62	  软错误内联 `color:#cc0000` 片段（source visible），硬错误 `.katex-error` class；
    63	  `math.css` 两条路径都 escalate 到 `--color-error` token 的 bordered box（spec D1 要求），不许 silent fallback
    64	
    65	## Wave 3 mdx-bridge 路由集成（pending）
    66	
    67	当前 `serializeMath` / `parseMath` 是 stub —— 暴露稳定签名，未被 mdx-bridge 消费。
    68	Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing table PR 会：
    69	
    70	1. 在 `mdx-bridge/parse.ts` 的 `mdastBlockToTiptap` 拦截 `mdxJsxFlowElement{name:'Math'}` →
    71	   `parseMath(node)` → 把返回 `MathTiptapNode` 嵌入 doc.content
    72	2. 在 `mdx-bridge/serialize.ts` 的 `tiptapToMdastBlock` 拦截 `type='math'` →
    73	   `serializeMath(node)` → 返回 `mdxJsxFlowElement` 进 mdast
    74	3. math block 无 children，递归不展开
    75	
    76	## Forward-compat consumers
    77	
    78	- **Z0 sample-blocks**: `<Math expression="\\int_0^1 x^2 dx" display />` 实例
    79	- **apps/site SSR**: import `@skb/block-math/ui-default/Math.astro`；consumer 同 PR 加 katex CSS import
    80	- **Wave 3 mdx-bridge integration**: 见上节
    81	- **Tiptap NodeView (editor-shell)**: 用 `MathEditorView`；NodeView wrapper 调 `renderMath` 直拿 HTML 字符串
    82	
    83	## Modifying this file
    84	
    85	`mathCore` 公共字段 (`name` / `kind` / `propsSchema` / `mdxComponent`) 与 `mathUiDefault`
    86	公共字段 (`coreName` / `uiId` / `EditorView` / `RenderView`) 变更需 ADR（与 block-foundation
    87	同 freeze 级别 —— 触发 mdx-bridge 路由 + sample-blocks 同步更新）。

    89	同步 Math.astro + Math.tsx 调用点（authority = ui-default/render-math.ts；ssr-render.test.ts 防漂移）。

/bin/bash -lc 'nl -ba packages/block-foundation/CONTRACT.md' in /home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# @skb/block-foundation Contract
     2	
     3	## Public surface
     4	
     5	- `BlockRegistry` class — 双层注册表（ADR-0003）
     6	  - `registerCore(core)` / `registerUI(ui)`
     7	  - `getCore(name)` / `listCores()`
     8	  - `getUI(coreName, uiId?)` / `listUIs(coreName)`
     9	- `defineCore(def)` / `defineUI(def)` factory helpers
    10	- `BlockCoreDefinition<T>` / `BlockUIDefinition<T>` / `BlockViewProps<T>` interfaces
    11	- `BlockKind = 'prose' | 'component' | 'render' | 'viz'` （四分类，Wave 2 由 2→4 additive 扩展）
    12	  - `'prose'` — Wave 1 default for headless prose blocks (无 MDX 组件，由 `proseExtensions` 提供 markdown 行为)
    13	  - `'component'` — Wave 1 for MDX-component blocks（block-callout / block-code / block-image — Track C simple-block-eng）
    14	  - `'render'` — **Wave 2 NEW** for hand-crafted render blocks consuming external runtime authority（block-math KaTeX / block-pdf react-pdf — Track D render-block-eng）
    15	  - `'viz'` — **Wave 2 NEW** for visualization blocks consuming heavy runtime libs（block-jupyter Pyodide / block-nn-viz TensorFlow.js / block-agent-flow React Flow — Track E viz-block-eng / kernel consumer）
    16	- `proseExtensions` — Tiptap 扩展数组，提供全部 markdown 行为
    17	
    18	## Invariants
    19	
    20	- `BlockKind` 四分不可破坏：新 block 必须明确归属 `prose` / `component` / `render` / `viz` 之一。Wave 1 仅 `prose` + `component` 二分；Wave 2 additive 扩展加 `render` + `viz`（由 `render-block-eng` 与 `viz-block-eng` 工种 own，与 agent-contract.md Tier 1 worker 分工一致）。kind 定义不可被进一步窄化（不允许把 `render` 重新归并入 `component`），扩展再加 kind 需 ADR
    21	- Prose blocks 零自写代码——任何看似需要新 prose block 的场景应通过组合 `proseExtensions` 内现有扩展或追加单条 Tiptap 扩展实现，不再加 prose-kind block
    22	- `BlockCoreDefinition.mdxComponent` 必须 PascalCase，且与 MDX 文件 import 中使用的名字一致
    23	- Core 与 UI 物理分离（ADR-0003）：core 不允许 import 任何 React/Tiptap 视觉 API；UI 必须 import core（不允许 inline 重复 schema）
    24	- 同 core 多 UI 时 `getUI(name)` 取首个注册：约定首个 uiId 为 `'default'`；adopter 在 register 顺序上需谨慎
    25	- **Defensive copy**：`listCores()` 与 `listUIs(coreName)` 每次返回 fresh array。Caller 修改返回值不影响 registry 内部 state。Implementation: `src/registry.ts:84` (`listCores`) + `src/registry.ts:96` (`listUIs`)；regression: `src/__tests__/registry.test.ts` "listCores returns a defensive copy" + "listUIs returns a defensive copy"
    26	- **Idempotent register**：`registerCore` 同 `name` 第二次调用 throws `Duplicate core name: <name>`；`registerUI` 同 `(coreName, uiId)` 第二次 throws `Duplicate UI registration: core=<coreName> uiId=<uiId>`。避免 silent override；实现见 `src/registry.ts:60-63` + `src/registry.ts:71-75`
    27	- **Schema strictness**：每个 `BlockCoreDefinition.propsSchema` 必须是 `z.object(...).strict()`；嵌套 `ZodObject` 同样必须 `.strict()`（[ADR-0006](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md) item #3 trip-hazard：`.strict()` 不传播）。当前类型仅约束到 `ZodTypeAny`（type-narrow 到 `ZodObject` 待 Wave 3）；Wave 2 行为约定 + reviewer + RFC.md §1 显式提醒。未 `.strict()` 不会 throw，但 `propsSchema.parse(input)` 会 silently strip unknown keys，引发 contract drift
    28	- **Serialize / parse hook ownership**：MDX serialize/parse 由各 `block-*` 包的 `core/` own（ADR-0003 D1+D2），不在 `block-foundation` 注册。`block-foundation` 仅 own `BlockCoreDefinition` 形状（含 `mdxComponent` 字符串）；`mdx-bridge` 通过 `mdxJsxFlowElement.name` 字符串路由到对应 block 的 export（命名约定 verb-as-prefix：`serialize<BlockName>` / `parse<BlockName>`，与 mdx-bridge 现有 `mdxToTiptap` / `tiptapToMdx` 同 style）。详见 RFC.md §"5. Serialize / parse hook ownership"
    29	
    30	## Forward-compat consumers (Wave 2+)
    31	
    32	Wave 2 中段当 `BlockRegistry` 开始用 `@skb/content-types` 的 schema 验证
    33	注册的 block props（如跨 block 共享的 frontmatter / metadata 形状），届时
    34	`@skb/content-types` 将被同 PR 加入 `package.json#dependencies` +
    35	`tsconfig.json#references`，并出现源码 `import { ... } from '@skb/content-types'`。
    36	
    37	当前 Wave 1 close 状态：源码无 content-types import，按
    38	[ADR-0008](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md) D1
    39	（dead-dep policy = tighten），dep 与 ref 均未声明；forward-compat 意图
    40	仅在本 prose 段表达，不在 package.json / tsconfig 占位。
    41	
    42	## How to register a new `block-*` package
    43	
    44	step-by-step walkthrough (core / ui-default / 测试样板 / 错误场景 / serialize-parse 归属)
    45	for the first real consumer 在 [RFC.md](./RFC.md) — 第一个 `block-*` PR
    46	(Task C1 / C2) 必须 reference 此 RFC；后续 `block-*` PR 沿用相同模式。
    47	RFC 是 consumer-facing 教程，CONTRACT.md 是 public API 形状 + 关键不变量
    48	的权威；二者补充而非重叠（[ADR-0008 D2](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md)）。
    49	
    50	## Modifying this file
    51	
    52	公共表面（`BlockCoreDefinition` / `BlockUIDefinition` / `BlockRegistry` / `BlockKind` / `BlockViewProps` / `defineCore` / `defineUI` / `proseExtensions`）的任何字段或方法增删改一律需 ADR — **包括添加 optional 字段、改 `proseExtensions` 数组（增删 Tiptap 扩展、顺序变更、`as const` shape 变化）**。spec §2.3 把 block-foundation 列为接口包，规则"改动需 ADR；全员同步"不区分 optional vs required。
    53	
    54	原因：optional 字段会传播到 mdx-bridge serialization + 8 个 Wave 2 block 的 core+ui 实现 + apps/site frontmatter schema；`proseExtensions` 改动直接影响 mdx-bridge round-trip fixtures + editor-shell parsing 行为；无 ADR 协调会引发漂移。
    55	
    56	## Related
    57	
    58	- 设计规格 §1.5 / §2.5（`../../docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-29-self-knowledge-base-design.md`）

     1	# RFC: How a `block-*` package registers with `@skb/block-foundation`
     2	
     3	> **Audience**: `simple-block-eng` / `render-block-eng` / `viz-block-eng` writing
     4	> `block-callout` / `block-code` / `block-image` / `block-math` / `block-pdf` /
     5	> `block-jupyter` / `block-nn-viz` / `block-agent-flow` cores; `ux-ui-lead`
     6	> writing the matching `ui-default/` halves.
     7	>
     8	> **Authority**: [ADR-0008](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md)
     9	> D2 freezes the registration interface as of 2026-04-30 (commit `b3581bc`).
    10	> This RFC is the consumer-facing walkthrough; the public API surface itself
    11	> lives in [`CONTRACT.md`](./CONTRACT.md).
    12	
    13	This RFC is **not** a spec — `CONTRACT.md` is. It is a step-by-step recipe
    14	showing how the first real consumer (`block-callout`, Task C1/C2) wires its
    15	core + UI into a `BlockRegistry`. Subsequent `block-*` PRs follow the same
    16	shape; cloned `block-code` / `block-image` cores (Tasks C3-C5b) inherit
    17	this structure verbatim.
    18	
    19	## 1. core/ side
    20	
    21	`core/` is the headless half (ADR-0003). No React, no Tiptap; just a Zod
    22	schema + identifying metadata. Place it at `packages/<block>/src/core/`
    23	and export through the package's `core` subpath.
    24	
    25	```typescript
    26	// packages/block-callout/src/core/core-definition.ts
    27	import { defineCore, type BlockCoreDefinition } from '@skb/block-foundation';
    28	import { z } from 'zod';
    29	
    30	const propsSchema = z
    31	  .object({
    32	    variant: z.enum(['note', 'tip', 'warning', 'danger']),
    33	    title: z.string().optional(),
    34	  })
    35	  .strict();
    36	
    37	export const calloutCore: BlockCoreDefinition<typeof propsSchema> = defineCore({
    38	  name: 'callout',
    39	  kind: 'component',
    40	  propsSchema,
    41	  mdxComponent: 'Callout',
    42	});
    43	```
    44	
    45	Notes that map directly to `BlockCoreDefinition` fields in
    46	[`src/registry.ts`](./src/registry.ts):
    47	
    48	- `name`: kebab-case, globally unique. Used as the registry key — duplicate
    49	  registration throws `Duplicate core name: <name>` at runtime.
    50	- `kind`: `'prose' | 'component' | 'render' | 'viz'` (4-way Wave 2 union; Wave 1
    51	  was binary `'prose' | 'component'` — additive expansion in Wave 2). Track C
    52	  simple-block-eng owns `'component'` (block-callout / block-code / block-image —
    53	  composable JSX with rich children); Track D render-block-eng owns `'render'`
    54	  (block-math / block-pdf — visual rendering of a pure declarative input via
    55	  external runtime authority like KaTeX or react-pdf); Track E viz-block-eng /
    56	  kernel consumer owns `'viz'` (block-jupyter / block-nn-viz / block-agent-flow —
    57	  interactive visualization with heavy runtime libs). `'prose'` is reserved for
    58	  behaviors covered by `proseExtensions` and is closed to new entries (CONTRACT
    59	  invariant: "Prose blocks 零自写代码").
    60	- `propsSchema`: must be a `z.object(...).strict()`; every nested
    61	  `ZodObject` must independently call `.strict()`. See
    62	  [`CONTRACT.md` § Invariants — Schema strictness](./CONTRACT.md#invariants)
    63	  + [ADR-0006](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md) item #3
    64	  (`.strict()` does not propagate into nested objects). The current type
    65	  signature `BlockCoreDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny>` is wider than
    66	  `ZodObject`; type-narrowing is deferred to Wave 3, so for Wave 2 this is
    67	  a behavior contract enforced by RFC + reviewer + integration tests, not
    68	  by TypeScript. Skipping `.strict()` does not throw — `propsSchema.parse`
    69	  silently strips unknown keys, which is exactly the contract-drift class
    70	  this rule prevents.
    71	- `mdxComponent`: PascalCase, must match the JSX tag used in `.mdx` files
    72	  exactly (the MDX bridge uses this string when serializing back). For
    73	  `block-callout`, MDX content reads `<Callout variant="note">…</Callout>`
    74	  so this field is `'Callout'`.
    75	
    76	`defineCore` is a no-op identity helper that exists purely to give TypeScript
    77	inference a hand — the returned object satisfies `BlockCoreDefinition<T>`
    78	with `T` inferred from `propsSchema`. Equivalent to writing the type
    79	annotation manually. `defineUI` (used in §2) is the symmetric helper for
    80	`BlockUIDefinition<T>`; both helpers exist for inference convenience only,
    81	so consumers can write `defineUI({ ... })` without manually spelling
    82	`<typeof calloutCore.propsSchema>`.
    83	
    84	## 2. ui-default/ side
    85	
    86	`ui-default/` is the presentational half. It imports the core (never inline-
    87	redefines its schema — see ADR-0006 item #4: "single-authority schema → audit
    88	every consumer for inline duplicates"), then attaches React components for
    89	the editor and renderer.
    90	
    91	```typescript
    92	// packages/block-callout/src/ui-default/index.ts
    93	import { defineUI, type BlockUIDefinition } from '@skb/block-foundation';
    94	import { calloutCore } from '../core/core-definition';
    95	import { CalloutEditorView } from './editor-view';
    96	import { CalloutRenderView } from './render-view';
    97	
    98	export const calloutUIDefault: BlockUIDefinition<
    99	  typeof calloutCore.propsSchema
   100	> = defineUI({
   101	  coreName: calloutCore.name, // must reference an already-registered core
   102	  uiId: 'default', // convention: first-registered UI is 'default'
   103	  EditorView: CalloutEditorView,
   104	  RenderView: CalloutRenderView,
   105	});
   106	```
   107	
   108	The two view components share the `BlockViewProps<TSchema>` shape from
   109	[`src/registry.ts`](./src/registry.ts):
   110	
   111	```typescript
   112	// packages/block-callout/src/ui-default/editor-view.tsx
   113	import type { ComponentType } from 'react';
   114	import type { BlockViewProps } from '@skb/block-foundation';
   115	import { calloutCore } from '../core/core-definition';
   116	
   117	export const CalloutEditorView: ComponentType<
   118	  BlockViewProps<typeof calloutCore.propsSchema>
   119	> = ({ props, content }) => {
   120	  // props.variant is z-inferred to 'note' | 'tip' | 'warning' | 'danger'
   121	  // props.title is z-inferred to string | undefined
   122	  // content is the optional Tiptap inner content as a string
   123	  return (
   124	    <aside data-variant={props.variant}>
   125	      {props.title ? <strong>{props.title}</strong> : null}
   126	      <div>{content}</div>
   127	    </aside>
   128	  );
   129	};
   130	```
   131	
   132	`RenderView` follows the same shape but renders for static output (Astro
   133	build / SSR path) rather than the editor. UX/visual conventions for the
   134	component itself (tokens, spacing, accessibility) are owned by `ux-ui-lead`
   135	via `packages/design-tokens/CONTRACT.md`; this RFC fixes only the
   136	registration plumbing.
   137	
   138	### Wiring at boot
   139	
   140	`registerCore` must precede `registerUI` for the same `coreName`; otherwise
   141	`registerUI` throws `Unknown core for UI registration: <coreName>`. The
   142	boot path (Wave 3 `editor-shell` / `apps/site` integration) does:
   143	
   144	```typescript
   145	import { BlockRegistry } from '@skb/block-foundation';
   146	import { calloutCore } from '@skb/block-callout/core';
   147	import { calloutUIDefault } from '@skb/block-callout/ui-default';
   148	
   149	const registry = new BlockRegistry();
   150	registry.registerCore(calloutCore);
   151	registry.registerUI(calloutUIDefault);
   152	
   153	// later, render-time lookup:
   154	const ui = registry.getUI('callout'); // returns calloutUIDefault (uiId='default')
   155	const ui2 = registry.getUI('callout', 'minimal'); // exact match by uiId
   156	```
   157	
   158	When multiple UIs are registered for one core, `getUI(name)` without a
   159	`uiId` returns the **first registered** (CONTRACT invariant — convention:
   160	register `'default'` first). `getUI(name, uiId)` does exact match.
   161	
   162	## 3. Consumer-side test template
   163	
   164	Each `block-*` package ships an integration test that wires its own core +
   165	UI into a fresh registry and verifies the round-trip. This is the minimum
   166	surface; package-specific tests (props validation, schema edge cases, view
   167	rendering) live alongside.
   168	
   169	```typescript
   170	// packages/block-callout/src/__tests__/registry-integration.test.ts
   171	import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
   172	import { BlockRegistry } from '@skb/block-foundation';
   173	import { calloutCore } from '../core/core-definition';
   174	import { calloutUIDefault } from '../ui-default';
   175	
   176	describe('block-callout registry integration', () => {
   177	  it('registers core + UI and resolves both', () => {
   178	    const reg = new BlockRegistry();
   179	    reg.registerCore(calloutCore);
   180	    reg.registerUI(calloutUIDefault);
   181	
   182	    expect(reg.getCore('callout')).toBe(calloutCore);
   183	    expect(reg.getUI('callout')).toBe(calloutUIDefault);
   184	    expect(reg.getUI('callout', 'default')).toBe(calloutUIDefault);
   185	  });
   186	
   187	  it('propsSchema accepts every declared variant', () => {
   188	    for (const variant of ['note', 'tip', 'warning', 'danger'] as const) {
   189	      const parsed = calloutCore.propsSchema.parse({ variant });
   190	      expect(parsed.variant).toBe(variant);
   191	    }
   192	  });
   193	
   194	  it('propsSchema rejects unknown variants and unknown keys', () => {
   195	    expect(() => calloutCore.propsSchema.parse({ variant: 'bogus' })).toThrow();
   196	    expect(() =>
   197	      calloutCore.propsSchema.parse({ variant: 'note', extra: 1 }),
   198	    ).toThrow(); // .strict() — extra key rejected
   199	  });
   200	});
   201	```
   202	
   203	The patterns mirror
   204	[`src/__tests__/registry.test.ts`](./src/__tests__/registry.test.ts) in this
   205	package — the foundation owns registry-mechanics tests; each `block-*` owns
   206	the integration test for its own core+UI pair.
   207	
   208	## 4. Error scenarios
   209	
   210	### 4.1 Runtime errors thrown by `BlockRegistry`
   211	
   212	These come from the methods in [`src/registry.ts`](./src/registry.ts). Workers
   213	should write tests asserting each one fires for their block where applicable.
   214	
   215	| Scenario                                        | Method        | Error message                                              |
   216	| ----------------------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
   217	| Register two cores with the same `name`         | `registerCore` | `Duplicate core name: <name>`                              |
   218	| `registerUI` before `registerCore` (same name)  | `registerUI`   | `Unknown core for UI registration: <coreName>`             |
   219	| Two UIs with same `(coreName, uiId)`            | `registerUI`   | `Duplicate UI registration: core=<coreName> uiId=<uiId>`   |
   220	| `getCore(name)` for an unregistered name        | `getCore`      | returns `undefined` (no throw)                             |
   221	| `getUI(coreName)` with no UIs registered        | `getUI`        | returns `undefined` (no throw)                             |
   222	| `getUI(coreName, uiId)` with non-matching uiId  | `getUI`        | returns `undefined` (no throw)                             |
   223	
   224	### 4.2 Compile-time errors (caught by `pnpm typecheck`)
   225	
   226	`BlockCoreDefinition` field errors:
   227	
   228	| Scenario                              | TypeScript error (representative)                                  |
   229	| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
   230	| `name` missing                        | `Property 'name' is missing in type ...`                           |
   231	| `name` not a `string`                 | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                    |
   232	| `kind` missing                        | `Property 'kind' is missing in type ...`                           |
   233	| `kind` not `'prose' \| 'component' \| 'render' \| 'viz'` | `Type '"<X>"' is not assignable to type 'BlockKind'`    |
   234	| `propsSchema` missing                 | `Property 'propsSchema' is missing in type ...`                    |
   235	| `propsSchema` not a Zod schema        | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'ZodTypeAny'`                |
   236	| `mdxComponent` missing                | `Property 'mdxComponent' is missing in type ...`                   |
   237	| `mdxComponent` not a `string`         | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                    |
   238	
   239	`BlockUIDefinition` field errors (symmetric to `BlockCoreDefinition`: 4 fields × {missing, wrong-type} = 8 rows + props-shape divergence):
   240	
   241	| Scenario                                                | TypeScript error (representative)                                                            |
   242	| ------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
   243	| `coreName` missing                                      | `Property 'coreName' is missing in type ...`                                                 |
   244	| `coreName` not a `string`                               | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                                              |
   245	| `uiId` missing                                          | `Property 'uiId' is missing in type ...`                                                     |
   246	| `uiId` not a `string`                                   | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                                              |
   247	| `EditorView` missing                                    | `Property 'EditorView' is missing in type ...`                                               |
   248	| `EditorView` not a `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<T>>`   | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>'`              |
   249	| `RenderView` missing                                    | `Property 'RenderView' is missing in type ...`                                               |
   250	| `RenderView` not a `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<T>>`   | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>'`              |
   251	| `props` shape passed to view diverges from `TSchema`    | `Type '<inferred>' does not satisfy ...`（出现在 view 实现内 destructure / use point）       |
   252	
   253	What TypeScript does NOT catch (covered by runtime throws above + integration test + reviewer):
   254	
   255	- `coreName` typo (it's a `string`; runtime `Unknown core for UI registration: ...` catches at boot)
   256	- `name` collision across packages (runtime `Duplicate core name: ...` catches at register time)
   257	- `mdxComponent` PascalCase casing (string-level; reviewer + integration test catches)
   258	- `propsSchema` is a Zod schema but **not** `z.object(...).strict()` (e.g. `z.string()`, `z.array(...)`, or `z.object(...)` without `.strict()`): the type signature only requires `ZodTypeAny`, so TypeScript accepts it; `propsSchema.parse(input)` does not throw but silently strips unknown keys, which is the contract-drift risk [`CONTRACT.md` § Invariants — Schema strictness](./CONTRACT.md#invariants) closes via reviewer + integration test (type-narrow to `ZodObject` deferred to Wave 3)
   259	
   260	### 4.3 Defensive-copy guarantee
   261	
   262	`listCores()` and `listUIs(coreName)` return fresh arrays per call (CONTRACT
   263	[Invariants](./CONTRACT.md#invariants) — "Defensive copy"). Consumers can
   264	freely mutate the returned arrays without leaking into the registry. Don't
   265	write tests that assume identity (`reg.listCores() === reg.listCores()`) —
   266	they're new arrays each time. Implementation: `src/registry.ts:84` + `:96`.
   267	
   268	## 5. Serialize / parse hook ownership (scope clarification)
   269	
   270	`block-foundation` does **not** own MDX serialize/parse. Each `block-*`
   271	package owns its own `core/serialize.ts` + `core/parse.ts`; `mdx-bridge`
   272	routes between them via the `mdxComponent` string when it encounters an
   273	`mdxJsxFlowElement` in a parsed MDX document. This split mirrors
   274	[ADR-0003 D1+D2](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0003-headless-presentational-split.md):
   275	"Headless 层 (block core) — props schema (Zod) + MDX serialize/parse + 业务逻辑（无 React 依赖）"
   276	lives in `core/`, not in the foundation.
   277	
   278	```
   279	@skb/block-foundation             owns: BlockCoreDefinition shape (incl. mdxComponent: string)
   280	                                  owns: BlockRegistry routing table
   281	                                  does NOT own: serialize / parse functions
   282	
   283	@skb/block-callout/core           owns: calloutCore (registers via mdxComponent='Callout')
   284	                                  owns: serializeCallout(node) → mdast (Wave 2 add)
   285	                                  owns: parseCallout(mdast) → TiptapNode (Wave 2 add)
   286	
   287	@skb/mdx-bridge                   does the routing:
   288	                                  - parse: mdast.mdxJsxFlowElement{name:'Callout'} → call parseCallout
   289	                                  - serialize: TiptapNode{type:'callout'} → call serializeCallout
   290	```
   291	
   292	**Naming convention for Wave 2 block-* packages**:
   293	
   294	```typescript
   295	// packages/block-callout/src/core/index.ts
   296	export { calloutCore } from './core-definition';
   297	export { serializeCallout } from './serialize'; // (TiptapNode input → mdast output)
   298	export { parseCallout } from './parse';         // (mdast input → TiptapNode output)
   299	```
   300	
   301	mdx-bridge will add a routing table (Wave 2 mdx-bridge change, not in this
   302	RFC) keyed by `mdxComponent` name → resolved by `coreName` → calls the
   303	block's exported `serialize<BlockName>` / `parse<BlockName>`. Until then,
   304	mdx-bridge throws on unknown `mdxJsxFlowElement` per its existing
   305	[Fail-loud rule](../mdx-bridge/CONTRACT.md#fail-loud-rule)
   306	(`mdx-bridge: unsupported {block,inline,mark} type "<type>". Add a
   307	fixture and a parse + serialize case before introducing this <kind>.`).
   308	
   309	This RFC fixes the **`block-foundation` registration shape**; the
   310	serialize/parse routing on the `mdx-bridge` side is a separate Wave 2
   311	mdx-bridge PR (not this Track A2 PR). Block authors implementing C1+
   312	should expose `serialize<Block>` / `parse<Block>` from `core/index.ts`
   313	following the convention above so mdx-bridge can wire them when the
   314	routing table lands.
   315

    10	- `.` (root barrel) — re-export `./core` + `./ui-default`
    11	- `./core` — headless 层
    12	  - `calloutCore: BlockCoreDefinition<typeof propsSchema>` — name=`'callout'` / kind=`'component'` / mdxComponent=`'Callout'`
    13	  - `serializeCallout(node) → mdxJsxFlowElement` — Tiptap → mdast (Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing 时启用)
    14	  - `parseCallout(mdast) → TiptapNode` — mdast → Tiptap (同上)
    15	  - 类型: `CalloutTiptapNode` / `CalloutMdastJsxElement`
    16	- `./ui-default` — presentational 层（Wave 2 Track C2）
    17	  - `calloutUIDefault: BlockUIDefinition<typeof calloutCore.propsSchema>` — coreName=`'callout'` / uiId=`'default'`
    18	  - `CalloutEditorView` / `CalloutRenderView` — `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<typeof calloutCore.propsSchema>>`，DOM 形状字节级一致（共享 `CalloutBody` 原语）
    19	  - `CalloutBody` — 视觉单一权威 primitive，editor + render 双视图都嵌入
    20	  - `VARIANT_TOKENS` — `Record<Variant, { label, accentToken: ColorTokenName }>` 共享视觉元数据
    21	  - `VARIANT_ICONS` — `Record<Variant, ComponentType>`，4 个手画 SVG（无 emoji，per ui-ux-pro-max `no-emoji-icons` rule）
    22	  - 类型: `Variant` (`'note' | 'tip' | 'warning' | 'danger'`)
    23	- `./ui-default/callout.css` — 视觉规则单一来源
    24	  - 选择器契约: 所有变体规则锁定在 `[data-callout-variant="…"]` 上（CalloutBody 总是 emit）
    25	  - 颜色完全来自 `@skb/design-tokens` CSS 变量（`var(--color-X)` 形式），不允许硬编码 hex / rgb 字面值
    26	  - 消费方 (`apps/site` / `editor-shell`) 必须在启动时 import 一次：`import '@skb/block-callout/ui-default/callout.css';`
    27	
    28	`propsSchema` 形状（见 `src/core/core-definition.ts`）：
    29	
    30	```typescript
    31	z.object({
    32	  variant: z.enum(['note', 'tip', 'warning', 'danger']),
    33	  title: z.string().optional(),
    34	}).strict()
    35	```
    36	
    37	## Invariants
    38	
    39	继承自 [block-foundation/CONTRACT.md](../block-foundation/CONTRACT.md#invariants)：
    40	`Defensive copy` / `Schema strictness` / `Default UI lookup` / `Serialize / parse hook ownership`
    41	（即 `BlockRegistry.listCores()` / `listUIs(coreName)` defensive-copy 行为、
    42	`propsSchema` 必须 `.strict()`、同 `coreName` 多 UI 时 `getUI(coreName)` 取首注册 `uiId`、
    43	serialize/parse 由本包 `core/` own 不在 foundation 注册）。
    44	
    45	block-callout 特定不变量：
    46	
    47	- **`coreName='callout'` (kebab-case)**: `calloutCore.name` 字面值固定，与 `BlockRegistry.registerCore`
    48	  注册键一致；后续 `block-code` / `block-image` 同 `coreName` kebab-case 约定
    49	- **`mdxComponent='Callout'` (PascalCase)**: 与 `<Callout variant=...>` JSX 标签一致；
    50	  mdx-bridge 通过 `mdxJsxFlowElement.name` 字符串路由（[block-foundation RFC §1](../block-foundation/RFC.md#1-core-side)）
    51	- **Serialize / parse hook 命名**: `serializeCallout` / `parseCallout`，verb-as-prefix
    52	  约定（[RFC §5](../block-foundation/RFC.md#5-serialize--parse-hook-ownership-scope-clarification)）
    53	- **`uiId='default'` reserved**: `calloutUIDefault.uiId === 'default'`；本包 inherit
    54	  foundation 的 `Default UI lookup` 不变量（首注册即 default）
    55	- **EditorView / RenderView byte-equivalent DOM**: 两视图都通过 `CalloutBody` 原语
    56	  渲染，DOM tree / class 名 / `data-callout-variant` / `role` / `aria-label`
    57	  完全一致；唯一差异是 `content` 缺省时 EditorView 显示 `.skb-callout-empty` 占位、
    58	  RenderView 直接渲染空 children。任何分歧都触发 `ui-default.test.tsx` byte-equiv 用例 fail
    59	- **CSS = single visual authority**: 视觉规则只在 `ui-default/callout.css`，
    60	  React 组件不带 inline `style`（happy-dom 测试拒绝 `rgb(var())` 字面值，所以
    61	  variant 颜色只能走 CSS data-attribute 选择器路径）；任何变体颜色变更都改 callout.css
    62	  + design-tokens 任一处即可，不必 touch React 树
    63	- **No emoji in icons**: `VARIANT_ICONS` 全部为 hand-traced SVG (24×24 stroke 1.75)，
    64	  per ui-ux-pro-max `no-emoji-icons` rule + spec §3.5
    65	- **Headless 自给**: `core/` 不 import 任何 React / Tiptap UI 模块；仅依赖
    66	  `@skb/block-foundation` 类型 + `zod`（ADR-0003 D1）
    67	- **propsSchema single authority**: 仅在 `src/core/core-definition.ts` 定义，
    68	  ui-default / serialize / parse / 测试均 import `calloutCore.propsSchema`，
    69	  不允许在 consumer 处 inline 重复（ADR-0006 item #4）
    70	- **Self-validating serialize/parse**: `serializeCallout` 调 `propsSchema.parse(node.attrs)`；
    71	  `parseCallout` 调 `propsSchema.parse(rawProps)`。任何契约偏离在 mdx-bridge 路由前已 throw
    72	- **Registry integration**: `registerCore(calloutCore)` + `getCore('callout')` round-trip —
    73	  see [`src/__tests__/registry-integration.test.ts`](src/__tests__/registry-integration.test.ts)
    74	
    75	## Wave 3 mdx-bridge 路由集成（pending）
    76	
    77	当前 `serializeCallout` / `parseCallout` 是 stub —— 暴露稳定签名，未被 mdx-bridge 消费。
    78	Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing table PR 会：
    79	
    80	1. 在 `mdx-bridge/parse.ts` 的 `mdastBlockToTiptap` 拦截 `mdxJsxFlowElement{name:'Callout'}` →
    81	   `parseCallout(node)` → 把返回 `CalloutTiptapNode` 嵌入 doc.content
    82	2. 在 `mdx-bridge/serialize.ts` 的 `tiptapToMdastBlock` 拦截 `type='callout'` →
    83	   `serializeCallout(node)` → 返回 `mdxJsxFlowElement` 进 mdast
    84	3. children 递归走 mdx-bridge 现有 inline / block 处理
    85	
    86	集成完成后，`__tests__/fixtures/` 下三组 fixture 移交给 `packages/mdx-bridge/__tests__/fixtures/`
    87	（编号 10-12）配 RTT 测试（`mdx-doctor` 强制 byte-equivalent 双向）。
    88	
    89	## Forward-compat consumers
    90	
    91	- **Wave 3 mdx-bridge integration**: 见上节
    92	- **Wave 2 后续 block packages**: `block-code` / `block-image` / `block-math` 等按本包
    93	  结构 clone (codex-block-generator template source)；`scripts/new-block.ts` 模板
    94	  source = `packages/block-callout/`。clone 时 ADR-0008 D1 由模板带过：
    95	  `package.json#dependencies` 含 `@skb/design-tokens`（type-only `ColorTokenName` import
    96	  in `variant-tokens.ts` 满足 dead-dep grep），`tsconfig.json#references` 含
    97	  `../design-tokens`，`callout.css` 模板 mirror visual rules under
    98	  `[data-<block>-variant]` 选择器
    99	
   100	## Modifying this file
   101	
   102	`calloutCore` 公共字段 (`name` / `kind` / `propsSchema` / `mdxComponent`) 的字段或值变更需 ADR
   103	（与 block-foundation/CONTRACT.md 同 freeze 级别 —— 改 propsSchema 形状 / 改 mdxComponent /
   104	改 kind 都触发 mdx-bridge 路由 + ui-default + sample-blocks 同步更新）。
   105	
   106	`serializeCallout` / `parseCallout` 签名变更需配 mdx-bridge routing PR 同步（强耦合）。

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# ADR-0008: Wave 2 entry policies — dead-dep + block-foundation interface freeze
     2	
     3	| 字段 | 值                                                                                                                                                                |
     4	| ---- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
     5	| 状态 | accepted                                                                                                                                                          |
     6	| 日期 | 2026-04-30                                                                                                                                                        |
     7	| 作者 | refactorer (Claude Opus 4.7 1M ctx)                                                                                                                               |
     8	| 触发 | structure-auditor Wave 1 close baseline §7 推荐 #1（dead-dep policy）+ #2（block-foundation interface freeze）；ADR-0002 D3 erratum #19（F1）+ #20（F2）+ #21（block-foundation RFC） |
     9	| 替代 | 不替代任何 ADR；扩展 [ADR-0001](ADR-0001-stack-selection.md) §1.2/§1.8 monorepo 规则 + [ADR-0003](ADR-0003-headless-presentational-split.md) D2 双层接口        |
    10	
    11	## Context
    12	
    13	`docs/audits/structure-2026-04-29-wave-1.md` §7 提出 3 个结构关注点；其中 #1
    14	（dead-dep policy）+ #2（block-foundation interface freeze）必须在 Wave 2 第一个
    15	`block-*` PR 之前 lock，否则会让 block-callout 入门 PR 同时承担"消费 contract"+
    16	"决议 dep policy" 双重负担，触发 R-round inflation（Wave 1 Track F 5 轮 + Track A
    17	3 轮的同源教训）。
    18	
    19	ADR-0002 D3 已记录两处 forward-compat dead-dep：
    20	
    21	- **F1 — `packages/mdx-bridge`** `package.json#dependencies` 列出
    22	  `@skb/block-foundation` + `@skb/content-types`，源码零 import；
    23	  `tsconfig.json#references` 留空（即 package.json 与 tsconfig 不一致）。
    24	- **F2 — `packages/block-foundation`** `package.json#dependencies` 列出
    25	  `@skb/content-types`，源码零 import；`tsconfig.json#references` 已含
    26	  `{ "path": "../content-types" }`（即 package.json 与 tsconfig 一致但都是 dead）。
    27	
    28	structure-auditor §7 推荐 #1 给出两条互斥路径：
    29	
    30	- **Option A（tighten）**：每个 `package.json#dependencies/peerDependencies` 中的
    31	  `@skb/*` 必须对应至少一个源码 `from '@skb/<pkg>'` import；
    32	  CONTRACT.md 可在 prose 段表达 forward-compat 意图，但 package.json 不占位。
    33	- **Option B（relax）**：package.json 可 forward-declare；
    34	  `tsconfig.json#references` 必须与 `package.json` 一致；CONTRACT.md 必须解释。
    35	
    36	structure-auditor §7 推荐 #2 提出 block-foundation MDX renderer interface freeze：
    37	Wave 2 brings the first `block-callout` package (per agent-contract `simple-block-eng`)
    38	作为 `block-foundation` 双层 API 第一个真实 consumer；任何接口 gap 会在
    39	block-callout review 阶段反复返工。
    40	
    41	## Decision
    42	
    43	### D1：dead-dep policy = Option A（tighten）
    44	
    45	**规则**：每个 `package.json#dependencies` / `peerDependencies` 中的 `@skb/*`
    46	工作区依赖**必须**对应至少一个源码 `from '@skb/<pkg>'` import。
    47	forward-compat 意图（"Wave N+1 will consume X"）可在 CONTRACT.md prose 段表达，
    48	但**不在 package.json 占位**。
    49	
    50	**理由（Option A 优于 Option B）**：
    51	
    52	1. **audit invariant 更简洁**：dead-dep = bug，结构审计不必为
    53	   "forward-compat 是合法 dead-dep" 维护特例；structure-auditor 月度可机械化
    54	   `grep` 检查（"package.json `@skb/*` ↔ source import 一一对应"），
    55	   比 CONTRACT 文本对比可靠。
    56	2. **YAGNI**：mdx-bridge / block-foundation 真用上时再加 dep 是同包改动 + 同 PR
    57	   单点 churn；预先占位换来的"少改一行 package.json"不抵 audit 复杂度。
    58	3. **CONTRACT prose 仍可表达意图**：authoring intent 不丢，只是不再让
    59	   `package.json` 同时承担 "runtime fact" + "future plan" 双重语义。
    60	4. **Phase 3 npm publish 反向证据弱**：当前 phase 全部 `private: true`
    61	   workspace 包，npm publish 顾虑是 Phase 3 议题；届时若需 forward-compat 的
    62	   package.json 形态可在 ADR-N 重新审视；当前不应被 hypothetical future
    63	   requirement 绑架（CLAUDE.md hard rule "Don't design for hypothetical future"）。
    64	
    65	**具体执行（D1 应用范围限于 Wave 1 已识别的两处）**：
    66	
    67	| 包                             | 改动                                                                                                                                              |
    68	| ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
    69	| `packages/mdx-bridge`          | 删 `package.json#dependencies` 的 `@skb/block-foundation` + `@skb/content-types`；`tsconfig.json` 已无相关 references，无需改；`CONTRACT.md` § "Wave 1 declared peers" 改 prose（描述 forward-compat 意图，不再宣称 deps 占位）。 |
    70	| `packages/block-foundation`    | 删 `package.json#dependencies` 的 `@skb/content-types`；删 `tsconfig.json#references` 中 `{ "path": "../content-types" }`（与 package.json 同步）；`CONTRACT.md` 加一段 prose 说明 Wave 2 引入 content-types 的时机。 |
    71	
    72	`structure-auditor` 月度审计（next: 2026-05）增加机械检查项：扫所有
    73	`packages/*/package.json` 的 `@skb/*` deps，与同包 `src/**/*.{ts,tsx}` 的
    74	`from '@skb/...'` import 比对，发现单边即报 violation。
    75	
    76	### D2：block-foundation 注册 API freeze（spec for first real consumer block-callout）
    77	
    78	在 `packages/block-foundation/RFC.md`（新增）写 walkthrough："block-callout
    79	如何注册 core + ui-default 到 BlockRegistry"，覆盖：
    80	
    81	- `registerCore(core)` + `propsSchema` + `mdxComponent` name + `serialize/parse` hook 形状
    82	- `registerUI(ui)` + `uiId` + `EditorView` / `RenderView` 类型契约
    83	- 错误场景（unknown core / 重复 uiId / propsSchema 非 ZodObject）的 throw 行为
    84	- consumer-side 测试样板（`block-callout/src/__tests__/registry-integration.test.ts` 雏形）
    85	
    86	第一个 `block-*` PR（C1: block-callout/core）必须 reference 此 RFC；后续
    87	`block-*` PR 沿用模式。**RFC.md 不是 CONTRACT.md**：CONTRACT.md 规定 public API
    88	形状（已存在），RFC.md 是 walkthrough（教 consumer 如何用）；二者补充而非重叠。
    89	
    90	D2 的实施在 Task A2（owner: `block-foundation-eng`，blocked_by Task A1=本 ADR commit）。
    91	本 ADR 仅 freeze D2 的**目标**与**承诺**；具体 RFC.md 文本由 Task A2 PR 落地。
    92	
    93	## Consequences
    94	
    95	### 正面
    96	
    97	- **audit invariant 简洁可机械化**：structure-auditor 月度增加单条扫描（package.json `@skb/*` ↔ source import），覆盖 D1 不变量。
    98	- **block-foundation 接口在 block-callout 入门前 freeze**：预防 Wave 1 R-round inflation 同源问题（教训源 ADR-0006 instances #1, #5）。
    99	- **YAGNI 原则在 monorepo 层落地**：dead-dep 不再合法，迫使依赖在真消费时声明。
   100	- **Wave 2 plan-draft 阻塞解锁**：Task A1 commit 后 Track C/D/E/G 全部解锁。
   101	
   102	### 负面
   103	
   104	- **若 Phase 3 npm publish 阶段需 forward-compat package.json 形态**（包发布前要把
   105	  optional peer 显式声明），届时需 ADR-N 重新评估；当前判断该顾虑不充分（私有
   106	  workspace 全程 `private: true`）。
   107	- **block-foundation tsconfig 短期失去 content-types reference**：在 D2/D3
   108	  block-callout 落地后会重建（block-callout 的 tsconfig refs 需要 content-types
   109	  + block-foundation；block-foundation 自己若 Wave 2 中段开始 import content-types
   110	  schema 用于 props 验证，再加 ref）。这是预期 churn，不是退化。
   111	
   112	### Risks (mitigated)
   113	
   114	- **风险**：Wave 2 中段 block-foundation 真消费 content-types 时漏加 dep + ref。
   115	  **缓解**：`pnpm typecheck` + structure-auditor 双门会立刻报缺失（import 找不到 / refs 不一致），与 Wave 1 同类问题处理流程一致。
   116	- **风险**：Option A 让"forward-compat 意图"只能进 CONTRACT prose，未来读者可能漏看。
   117	  **缓解**：每个 block-* PR review chain 已强制读 CONTRACT.md（ADR-0007 D2 row 1 触发），prose 段是 review 必经；review 也会检查 dep 与 import 一致。
   118	
   119	## Implementation
   120	
   121	按 ADR-0006 D8 staging protocol（authority + generated/consumed surface 同 bundle staged）：
   122	
   123	1. **同 bundle commit（本 ADR + 应用同 PR）**：
   124	   - `docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md`（本 ADR 自身）
   125	   - `docs/decisions/README.md`（索引加 ADR-0008 行）
   126	   - `packages/mdx-bridge/package.json`（删 2 条 dead deps）
   127	   - `packages/mdx-bridge/CONTRACT.md` § "Wave 1 declared peers" 改 prose
   128	   - `packages/block-foundation/package.json`（删 1 条 dead dep）
   129	   - `packages/block-foundation/tsconfig.json`（删 references content-types）
   130	   - `packages/block-foundation/CONTRACT.md`（加 prose 说明 Wave 2 真消费时机）
   131	   - `pnpm-lock.yaml`（如 `pnpm install` 后有变动 — workspace dep 删除通常无 lockfile 影响，但 ADR-0006 D8 sub-form C 要求 verify）
   132	2. **后续 PR**（Task A2，blocked_by 本 commit）：
   133	   - 新增 `packages/block-foundation/RFC.md`
   134	   - `packages/block-foundation/CONTRACT.md` 加一行指向 RFC.md
   135	3. **structure-auditor 2026-05 月度审计**：增加 D1 机械扫描项；任何 dead-dep
   136	   再现 → 直接报 violation 阻断 PR。
